Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in classical_mechanics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 313 and the invariant I(A,B) = 113 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the classical_mechanics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 313 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 113. QED.
